# What the filter costs on a board (#41), pinned from what one measured; `data/onboard.sh pin`
# prints these lines, and validation/cost.md renders them through `onboard` placeholders.
#
#   <build>/<warm|cold>            the firmware's build line: commit, compiler, opt-level, LTO,
#                                  CPU, FPU, clock, cache mode, flush-to-zero
#   <build>/<mode>/<run>           one trace's calls: per call, per call and outcome, per label
#   <build>/<mode>/bound           every run of that build together
#   <build>/<mode>/common          the runs every build was timed on, for comparing builds
#   <build>/<mode>/fmodf           `wrap_pi`'s reduction across the exponent gap, cycles
#
# Keys are `<group>.<stat>`: `n`, `min`, `mean`, `max` and their `_us` at `sysclk`, in cycles net
# of the least a `nop` took; `stack`, bytes painted beneath `Machine::execute` and the arm a call
# goes through, and `stack_raw` beneath the call into the dispatcher; `denormal`, calls that
# read a denormal input. `tools/onboard.py` owns how each is taken.
#
# Not checked in CI, which has no board: a figure here is a measurement of the commit its build
# line names, re-measured by running data/onboard.sh again, never edited by hand.
